Government Notifications

(CHAPTER 252) MUNICIPAL COUNCILS ORDINANCE

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under Sections 2 and 5 of Municipal Councils Ordinance (Chapter 252) and Section 27Fof Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) to be read with section 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), IFaiszer Musthapha, Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government, do by this order, amend the order published inthe Gazette Extraordinary No. 329/8 of 10th August, 1978 relating to the Colombo Municipal Council as follows :

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

"(4) Determine that the Colombo Municipal Council shall consist of such number of members to be elected in terms offollowing i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

i. Sixty six members to be elected under paragraph (a) of subsection (1) of section 5 of the Municipal CouncilsOrdinance (Chapter 252) ;

ii. Maximum of nineteen members to be retained under paragraph (b) of subsection (1) of section 5 of the MunicipalCouncils Ordinance (Chapter 252) ;

iii. Twenty eight female members to be elected under sub section (3) of section 27F of Local Authorities ElectionsOrdinance (Chapter 262)"

This order shall come into operation on the 01st day of July 2017.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,07th of April 2017.

PG 3493 - 1610 (2017/04)

(CHAPTER 252) MUNICIPAL COUNCILS ORDINANCE

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under sections 2 and 5 of Municipal Councils Ordinance (Chapter 252) and section 27Fof Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) to be read with section 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), IFaiszer Musthapha, Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government, do by this order, amend the order published inthe Gazette Extraordinary No. 329/8 of 10th August, 1978 relating to the Dehiwala-Mt. Lavinia Municipal Council asfollows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

"(4) Determine that the Dehiwala-Mt. Lavinia Municipal Council shall consist of such number of members to beelected in terms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

i. Twenty nine members to be elected under paragraph (a) of subsection (1) of section 5 of the Municipal CouncilsOrdinance (Chapter 252) ;

ii. Maximum of eight members to be retained under paragraph (b) of subsection (1) of section 5 of the MunicipalCouncils Ordinance (Chapter 252) ;

iii. Twelve female members to be elected under sub section (3) of section 27F of Local Authorities Elections Ordinance(Chapter 262)"

This order shall come into operation on the 01st day of July 2017.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,07th of April 2017.

PRADESHIYA SABHA ACT, No. 15 OF 1987

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under sections 1 and 6 of Pradeshiya Sabha Act and section 27F of Local AuthoritiesElections Ordinance (Chapter 262) to be read with section 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I Faiszer Musthapha,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government, do by this order, amend the order published in the Gazette ExtraordinaryNo. 453/5 of 12th May, 1987 relating to the Homagama Pradeshiya Sabha as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

"(4) Determine that the Homagama Pradeshiya Sabha shall consist of such number of members to be elected in termsof following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

i. Twenty eight members to be elected under paragraph (a) of subsection (1) of section 4 of the Pradeshiya SabhaAct, No. 15 of 1987 ;

ii. Maximum of eight members to be retained under paragraph (b) of subsection (1) of section 4 of the PradeshiyaSabha Act, No. 15 of 1987 ;

iii. Twelve female members to be elected under sub section (3) of section 27F of Local Authorities Elections Ordinance(Chapter 262)"

This order shall come into operation on the 01st day of July 2017.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,07th of April 2017.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under Sections 1 and 6 of Pradeshiya Sabha Act and Section’27F of Local AuthoritiesElections Ordinance (Chapter 262) to be read with Section 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I Faiszer Musthapha,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government, do by the Order, amend the Order, which was fimally amended by theGazette Extraordinary No. 1778/6 of 02nd October, 2012, published in the Gazette Extraordinary No. 453/7 of 12th May, 1987relating to the Bandaragama Pradeshiya Sabha as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph;

“(4) Determine that the Bandaragama Pradeshiya Sabha shall consist of such number of members to be elected in termsof following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

i.Twenty members to be elected under paragraph (a) of subsection (1) of Section 4 of the Pradeshiya Sabha Act, No.15 of 1987;

ii.Maximum of six members to be retained under paragraph (b) of subsection (1) of Section 4 of the PradeshiyaSabha Act, No. 15 of 1987;

iii.Eight female members to be elected under sub Section (3) of Section 27F of Local Authorities Elections Ordinance(Chapter 262)”

This Order shall come into operation on the 01st day of July 2017.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,07th day of April, 2017.

(CHAPTER 255) URBAN COUNCILS ORDINANCE

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under sections 2 and 5 of Urban Councils Ordinance (Chapter 255) and section 27Fof Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) to be read with section 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter2), I Faiszer Musthapha, Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government, do by this order, amend the order publishedin the Gazette Extraordinary No. 329/8 of 10th August, 1978 relating to the Ambalangoda Urban Council as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Ambalangoda Urban Council shall consist of such number of members to be elected interms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

i. Twelve members to be elected under paragraph (a) of subsection (1) of section 5 of the Urban CouncilsOrdinance (Chapter 255) ;

ii. Maximum of Three members to be retained under paragraph (b) of subsection (1) of section 5 of the UrbanCouncils Ordinance (Chapter 255) ;

iii. Five Female members to be elected under sub section (3) of section 27F of Local Authorities Elections Ordinance(Chapter 262)”

This order shall come into operation on the 01st day of July 2017.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,07th of April 2017.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.Colombo,07th day of April, 2017.

(CHAPTER 255) URBAN COUNCILS ORDINANCE

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under sections 2 and 5 of Urban Councils Ordinance (Chapter 255) and section 27Fof Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) to be read with section 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter2), I Faiszer Musthapha, Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government, do by this order, amend the order publishedin the Gazette Extraordinary No. 329/8 of 10th August, 1978 relating to the Tangalle Urban Council as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Tangalle Urban Council shall consist of such number of members to be elected in termsof following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

i. Eleven members to be elected under paragraph (a) of subsection (1) of section 5 of the Urban CouncilsOrdinance (Chapter 255) ;

ii. Maximum of three members to be retained under paragraph (b) of subsection (1) of section 5 of the UrbanCouncils Ordinance (Chapter 255) ;

iii. Four female members to be elected under subsection (3) of section 27F of Local Authorities Elections Ordinance(Chapter 262)”

This order shall come into operation on the 01st day of July 2017.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,07th day of April 2017.

ii.Maximum of three members to be retained under paragraph (b) of subsection (1) of Section 4 of the PradeshiyaSabha Act, No. 15 of 1987;

iii.Four female members to be elected under subsection (3) of section 27F of Local Authorities ElectionsOrdinance (Chapter 262)”

This order shall come into operation on the 01st day of July, 2017.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,07th day of April, 2017.

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under Sections 1 and 6 of Pradeshiya Sabha Act and Section 27F of Local AuthoritiesElections Ordinance (Chapter 262) to be read with Section 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I, Faiszer Musthapha,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government, do by the order, amend the order published in the Gazette ExtraordinaryNo. 453/19 of 12th May, 1987 relating to the Thirukkovil Pradeshiya Sabha as follows :

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Thirukkovil Pradeshiya Sabha shall consist of such number of members to be elected interms of following (i), (ii) and (iii) sub paragraphs :

(i) Ten members to be elected under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of the PradeshiyaSabha Act, No. 15 of 1987 ;

(ii) Maximum of Three members to be retained under paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of thePradeshiya Sabha Act, No. 15 of 1987 ;

(iii) Four Female members to be elected under Sub-section (3) of Section 27F of Local Authorities ElectionsOrdinance (Chapter 262)”.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

CHAPTER (255) URBAN COUNCILS ORDINANCE

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under sections 2 and 5 of Urban Councils Ordinance (Chapter 255) and Section 27F ofLocal Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) to be read with Section 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), IFaiszer Musthapha, Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government, do by this order, amend the order published inthe Gazette Extraordinary No. 329/8 of 10th August, 1978 relating to the Chilaw Urban Council, as follows :

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

I fldgi ( ^I& fPoh - YS% ,xld m%cd;dka;s%l iudcjd§ ckrcfha w;s úfYI .eiÜ m;%h - 2017'04'07PART I : SEC. (I) - GAZETTE EXTRAORDINARY OF THE DEMOCRATIC SOCIALIST REPUBLIC OF SRI LANKA - 07.04.2017155A

“(4) Determine that the Chilaw Urban Council shall consist of such number of members to be elected in terms offollowing i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

(i) Twelve members to be elected under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (1) of Section 5 of the UrbanCouncils Ordinance (Chapter 255) ;

(ii) Maximum of Three members to be retained under paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 5 of theUrban Councils Ordinance (Chapter 255) ;

(iii) Five Female members to be elected under Sub-section (3) of Section 27F of Local Authorities ElectionsOrdinance (Chapter 262)”

This order shall come into operation on the 01st day of July 2017.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,07th day of April, 2017.

PRADESHIYA SABHA ACT, No. 15 OF 1987

Order

BY virtue of the powers vested in me under sections 1 and 6 of Pradeshiya Sabha Act and Section 27F of Local AuthoritiesElections Ordinance (Chapter 262) to be read with Section 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I Faiszer Musthapha,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government, do by the order, amend the order published in the Gazette ExtraordinaryNo. 453/22 of 12th May, 1987 relating to the Anamaduwa Pradeshiya Sabha as follows :

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Anamaduwa Pradeshiya Sabha shall consist of such number of members to be elected interms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

(i) Eighteen members to be elected under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of the PradeshiyaSabha Act, No. 15 of 1987 ;

(ii) Maximum of Five members to be retained under paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 4 of thePradeshiya Sabha Act, No. 15 of 1987 ;

(iii) Seven Female members to be elected under Sub-section (3) of Section 27F of Local AuthoritiesElections Ordinance (Chapter 262)”

This order shall come into operation on the 01st day of July 2017.

FAISZER MUSTHAPHA,Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,07th day of April, 2017.

Order

“(4) Determine that the Anuradhapura Municipal Council shall consist of such number of members to be electedin terms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

(i) Fifteen members to be elected under paragraph (a) of Sub-section (1) of Section 5 of the MunicipalCouncils Ordinance (Chapter 252) ;

(ii) Maximum of four members to be retained under paragraph (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 5 of theMunicipal Councils Ordinance (Chapter 252) ;

(iii) Six female members to be elected under Sub-section (3) of Section 27F of Local Authorities ElectionsOrdinance (Chapter 262)”

(Chapter 252) Municipal Councils Ordinance

Order

By virtue of the powers vested in me, under Sections 2 and 5 of Municipal Councils Ordinance (Chapter 252) and Section 27F of Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262) to be read with Section 18 of Interpretation Ordinance (Chapter 2), I, Faiszer Musthapha, Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government, do by this order, amend the order published in the Gazette Extraordinary No. 329/8 of 10th August, 1978 relating to the Badulla Municipal Council as follows:

1. By the substitution for paragraph (4) thereof, of the following paragraph ;

“(4) Determine that the Badulla Municipal Council shall consist of such number of members to be elected in terms of following i, ii and iii sub paragraphs.

(i) Fifteen members to be elected under paragraph (a) of subsection (1) of Section 5 of the Municipal Councils Ordinance (Chapter 252); (ii) Maximum of four members to be retained under paragraph (b) of subsection (1) of Section 5 of the Municipal Councils Ordinance (Chapter 252); (iii) Six Female members to be elected under subsection (3) of Section 27F of Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262)”

This order shall come into operation on the 01st day of July, 2017.

Faiszer Musthapha,

Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,07th April, 2017.

This order shall come into operation on the 01st day of July, 2017.

Faiszer Musthapha,

Minister of Provincial Councils and Local Government.

Colombo,07th April, 2017.
